## authentik (`authentik`)

Pinned spec sha256 `dced8129a58b9b661f6c87b7d4d1129745c89ff33c34a7ca53d76ee5a94f556d`.

| Operation | Status | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- |
| core_tokens_view_key_retrieve | excluded | A GET (read grant) that returns a live API token key. Reading must never reveal credentials; view token keys in the admin interface. |
| endpoints_agents_enrollment_tokens_view_key_retrieve | excluded | A GET (read grant) that returns a live device enrollment token key. Reading must never reveal credentials; view it in the admin interface. |
| managed_blueprints_create | excluded | A blueprint instance with inline content is applied by the server as soon as it is saved (arbitrary model changes and expressions), the same code path as apply. |
| managed_blueprints_update | excluded | Replacing a blueprint instance's content makes the server apply it (arbitrary model changes and expressions), the same code path as apply. |
| managed_blueprints_partial_update | excluded | Editing a blueprint instance's content makes the server apply it (arbitrary model changes and expressions), the same code path as apply. |
| managed_blueprints_apply_create | excluded | Applies a blueprint to the instance: arbitrary model changes and expressions evaluated by the authentik server. Apply blueprints from the admin interface or the blueprint files on the host. |
| tasks_schedules_send_create | excluded | Runs a system schedule immediately (blueprint discovery, outpost controller, cleanup jobs) on the server, whichever schedule id is given. Trigger schedules from the admin interface. |
| tasks_tasks_retry_create | excluded | Re-executes a previously failed system task on the server, whichever task id is given, including ones that apply blueprints. Retry tasks from the admin interface. |

## Chatwoot (`chatwoot`)

Pinned spec sha256 `775d835239f9a0b8270afb03e5700f6435dfac370655fdc811e3230cc34d6ef8`.

| Operation | Status | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- |
| get-sso-url-of-a-user | excluded | Mints a one-time login URL that signs in as any user. A GET is a read grant, so it would let a read-only agent impersonate users; create SSO links from the Super Admin console instead. |
| getConversationMessages | excluded | Not a real route: the upstream swagger lists it as /accounts/{account_id}/conversations/{conversation_id}/messages without the /api/v1 prefix, and Rails routes it only under /api/v1 (listed as list-all-messages). The call would 404. |

## Forgejo (`forgejo`)

Pinned spec sha256 `f266cd74476051c4c45fb0ce6d5ef9a76aa10046a5b69c100257b6e16ce834de`.

| Operation | Status | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- |
| activitypubInstanceActorInbox | excluded | ActivityPub inbox for remote servers: it accepts HTTP-signature deliveries from federated actors, not API-token calls, so the connector cannot use it. |
| activitypubRepositoryInbox | excluded | ActivityPub inbox for remote servers: it accepts HTTP-signature deliveries from federated actors, not API-token calls, so the connector cannot use it. |
| activitypubPersonInbox | excluded | ActivityPub inbox for remote servers: it accepts HTTP-signature deliveries from federated actors, not API-token calls, so the connector cannot use it. |
| adminCronRun | excluded | Runs a server maintenance task (repository garbage collection, cleanup, sync jobs) on the Forgejo host at once, for any task. Start tasks from the admin dashboard. |
| adminCreateHook | excluded | Creates a server-wide (system) webhook that receives the events of every repository and organization. Too broad to hand to an agent; create it from the admin settings. |
| adminEditHook | excluded | Edits a server-wide (system) webhook that receives the events of every repository and organization; change it from the admin settings. |
| repoEditGitHook | excluded | Writes a server-side git hook script that the Forgejo host executes on every push to the repository: host code execution. Edit hooks over SSH or in the app. |
| userGetTokens | excluded | Token management requires the account password over HTTP Basic, and the connector authenticates with a token, so the call would always be refused; manage tokens in the app settings. |
| userCreateToken | excluded | Token creation requires the account password over HTTP Basic, and the connector authenticates with a token, so the call would always be refused; create tokens in the app settings. |
| userDeleteAccessToken | excluded | Token deletion requires the account password over HTTP Basic, and the connector authenticates with a token, so the call would always be refused; delete tokens in the app settings. |
